# S.I. No. 158/1973 - Imposition of Duties (No. 207) (Customs Duties and Form of Customs Tariff) Order, 1973

> **This is the law as enacted. Amendments are not incorporated below — each one is
> a separate file in this repository and a commit in this file's history.**

S.I. No. 158 of 1973.

IMPOSITION OF DUTIES (No. 207) (CUSTOMS DUTIES AND FORM OF CUSTOMS TARIFF) ORDER, 1973.

The Government, in exercise of the powers conferred on them by [section 1](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1957/en/act/pub/0007/sec0001.html#sec1) of the [Imposition of Duties Act, 1957](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1957/en/act/pub/0007/index.html) (No. 7 of 1957), and [section 22](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1962/en/act/pub/0015/sec0022.html#sec22) of the [Finance Act, 1962](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1962/en/act/pub/0015/index.html) (No. 15 of 1962), hereby order as follows:

###### 1.

1. This Order may be cited as the Imposition of Duties (No. 207) (Customs Duties and Form of Customs Tariff) Order, 1973.

###### 2.

2. This Order shall come into operation on the 1st day of July, 1973.

###### 3.

3. References in this Order to an Act or Order shall be construed as references to that Act or Order as amended by any subsequent Act or Order.

###### 4.

4. The rates of duty mentioned in the Schedule to this Order shall be in addition to and not in substitution for any compensatory amount levied by virtue of Articles 47 and 55 of the Act annexed to the Treaty of Accession of the State to the European Communities, signed at Brussels on the 22nd day of January, 1972.

###### 5.

5. The Imposition of Duties (No. 200) (Customs and Excise Duties and Form of Tariff) Order, 1972 ( [S.I. No. 220 of 1972](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1972/en/si/0220.html) ), is hereby amended by—

- (1) the substitution of "°C/degree Celsius" and "°F/degree Fahrenheit" for "C/Centigrade" and "F/Fahrenheit" respectively in the first and second columns of the Table at paragraph 3 (i),

- (2) the substitution of "£0·0007" for "£0·001" in paragraphs 18 (5) (*a*) and 18 (5) (*b*),

- (3) the substitution of "4%" for "6%" in paragraph 19 (3),

- (4) the substitution of "£0·004" for "£0·006" in paragraph 20 (2),

- (5) the substitution of "£2·654" for "£3·813" in paragraph 21 (2),

- (6) the substitution of "4%" for "6%" in paragraph 22 (*a*),

- (7) the substitution of "£2·384" for "£3·576" in paragraph 23 (2),

- (8) the substitution, for the rates specified in the eighth and ninth columns, respectively, of the Schedule to that Order, at each tariff code number mentioned in a column headed "Tariff Code No." in the Schedule to this Order, of the rates specified in the columns headed "United Kingdom rate" and "Northern Ireland rate", respectively, in that Schedule opposite the mention in the said column headed "Tariff Code No." of that tariff code number,

- (9) the substitution of "6·4% or £0·04 each*" for "9·6% or £0·06 each*" wherever it occurs in the provision set forth in Chapter 39 of the Schedule in relation to fixtures and fittings falling in tariff code numbers 3907-156, 3907-470 and 3907-577 which include one or more than one fluorescent tube of a type classified at tariff code number 8520-353,

- (10) the substitution of "0·8%" and "0·4%" respectively for "1·2%" and "0·8%" in paragraph 1 of the Special Provisions relating to the Duties on Paper and Paperboard in Chapter 48 of the Schedule,

- (11) the substitution of "6·4% or £0·04 each*" for "9·6% or £0·06 each*" wherever it occurs in the provision set forth in Chapter 70 of the Schedule in relation to fixtures and fittings falling in tariff code number 7014-587 which include one or more than one fluorescent tube of a type classified in tariff code number 8520-353,

- (12) the substitution of "6·4% or £0·04 each*" for "9·6% or £0·06 each*" wherever it occurs in the provision set forth in Chapter 83 of the Schedule in relation to fixtures and fittings falling in tariff code numbers 8307-316, 8307-340 and 8307-356 which include one or more than one fluorescent tube of a type classified in tariff code number 8520-353,

- (13) the substitution of "£0·04", "£0·016" and "£0·112" respectively for "£0·06", "£0·024" and "£0·168" wherever they occur in the provision set forth in Chapter 85 of the Schedule in relation to articles falling in tariff code number 8501-324 and 8501-372 which incorporate any transformer, inductor (choke), coil, wire-wound resistor or cabinet or other like container,

- (14) the substitution of "£0·08", "£0·04", "£0·016" and "£0·112", respectively, for "£0·12", "£0·06", "£0·032" and "£0·224" wherever they occur in the provision set forth in Chapter 85 of the Schedule in relation to articles falling in tariff code number 8515-284 which incorporate any loudspeaker, transformer, inductor (choke), coil, wire-wound resistor, capacitor with moveable vanes or cabinet or other like container,

- (16) the insertion in the Schedule at tariff heading number 34·05 before "Polishing preparations for furniture, woodwork and floors:" of the following:—

###### 6.

6. (1) This paragraph applies to manufactured tobacco which was manufactured in, and consigned from, the United Kingdom and was manufactured therein from materials other than materials falling within tariff heading number 24.02 in the Schedule to the Imposition of Duties (No. 200) (Customs and Excise Duties and Form of Tariff) Order, 1972 ( [S.I. No. 220 of 1972](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1972/en/si/0220.html) ).

- (2) In lieu of the duty of customs chargeable immediately before the commencement of this Order on tobacco to which this paragraph applies, there shall be charged, levied and paid on such tobacco a duty of customs at the several rates specified in the Schedule to this paragraph.

- (3) The provisions of section 8 of the Finance Act, 1919, shall apply to the duties imposed by this paragraph—

    - (i) with the substitution of "the area of application of the Acts of the Oireachtas" for "Great Britain and Ireland" and as though "manufactured tobacco" in the first column of the Second Schedule to that Act did not include manufactured tobacco to which this paragraph applies,

    - (ii) as though manufactured tobacco to which this paragraph applies, together with the descriptions of such manufactured tobacco in the Schedule to this paragraph, were mentioned separately in the said first column and the appropriate preferential rates specified in that Schedule were mentioned in the second column of the said Second Schedule opposite the mention of those goods in the first column thereof, and

    - (iii) as if the sentence beginning "Goods shall not be deemed" in subsection (1) were deleted.

- (4) In this paragraph "the United Kingdom" means Great Britain, Northern Ireland, the Isle of Man and the Channel Islands.

- (5)
    - (*a*) In the Schedule to this paragraph "other pipe tobacco" means manufactured tobacco (other than hard pressed tobacco) of kinds normally intended to be used in pipes.

    - (*b*) In this subparagraph and the Schedule to this paragraph "hard pressed tobacco" has the same meaning as it has in [section 17](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1940/en/act/pub/0014/sec0017.html#sec17) of the [Finance Act, 1940](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1940/en/act/pub/0014/index.html) (No. 14 of 1940).

###### 8.

8. (1) [Section 4](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1962/en/act/pub/0015/sec0004.html#sec4) (3) of the [Finance Act, 1962](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1962/en/act/pub/0015/index.html) (No. 15 of 1962), as amended by the Order of 1970, shall have effect, in respect of spirits used in medical preparations to which Article 2, Article 3 and the Protocol apply, as if, for the rates of £1.020 and £0·758 specified in that section, there were substituted the rates of £0·994 and £0·737, respectively.

- (2) [Section 36](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1969/en/act/pub/0021/sec0036.html#sec36) of the [Finance Act, 1969](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1969/en/act/pub/0021/index.html) , as amended by the Order of 1970, shall have effect, in respect of hydrocarbon oils to which Article 2 applies, as if—

    - (*a*) for the rate of £0·2117 specified in subsection (2) there were substituted a rate of £0·2075, and

    - (*b*) for the rate of £0·1815 specified in subsections (4) and (5) there were substituted, in each place where it occurs in those subsections, a rate of £0·1773.

- (3) In this paragraph—

"Article 2" means Article 2 of the Agreement between the European Economic Community and the Kingdom of Nor way signed at Brussels on the 14th day of May, 1973;

"Article 3" means Article 3 of the Agreement between the European Economic Community and the Republic of Cyprus signed at Brussels on the 19th day of December, 1972;

"the Order of 1970" means the Imposition of Duties (No. 187) (Customs and Excise Duties and For m of Tariff) Order, 1970 ( [S.I. No. 268 of 1970](https://www.irishstatutebook.ie/1970/en/si/0268.html) );

"the Protocol" means the Protocol laying down certain provisions relating to the Agreement establishing an association between the European Economic Community and the Republic of Cyprus consequent on the accession of new member States to the European Community, signed at Brussels on the 19th day of December, 1972.

###### 9.

GIVEN under the Official Seal of the Government this 19th day of June, 1973.

**LIAM MAC COSGAIR,**

Taoiseach.

**EXPLANATORY NOTE.**

The effects of the Order are: —

- (i) to make certain reductions in customs duties on goods of United Kingdom origin in accordance with the provisions of the Anglo-Irish Free Trade Area Agreement;

- (ii) to maintain in operation the special tariff concessions for certain goods of Northern Ireland origin;

- (iii) to convert the units of quantity at a large number of tariff headings to metric terms; and

- (iv) to make a number of other changes of a minor nature in the Customs tariff.
